  • AGI还要多久？一个国际知名 AI KOL 的真实困惑 · 乔木博客 🎙️ 向阳乔木 4/28/2026
    🌐 tech-engineering 🏷️ tech-trends
    📄 Dwarkesh Patel argues that continuous learning is the key bottleneck for AGI, not raw intelligence. Current LLMs fail to learn persistently like humans, hindering their ability to become true long-term employees. While reasoning capabilities are improving, the lack of adaptive learning prevents AI from automating many white-collar jobs. He predicts significant AI capabilities by 2028 and true continuous learning by 2032, emphasizing that solving continuous learning could lead to rapid AGI development.

    🌐 tech-engineering 🏷️ tech-trends
    📄 本文回顾了曾风靡全球的大数据时代。作者指出，大数据时代并未如预言般迎来指数级数据增长和完全由数据驱动的商业模式，许多企业的大数据系统沦为数据仓库。原因包括：高估了数据规模、实际有用数据少、硬件性能追赶、决策周期缩短导致旧数据价值降低，以及领导层不愿完全交出决策权。尽管如此，大数据时代留下了宝贵的技术和商业模式遗产，失败的经验也为未来的技术热潮提供了冷静判断的依据。
